Problems & How to Fix

— 75/100

First Contentful Paint

Result: 3.8 s

Why: The first visible content is taking 3.8 s to appear. This can make the page feel slow when users first open it.

How to fix: Reduce render-blocking CSS and JavaScript, optimize above-the-fold images, preload important resources, and remove unnecessary third-party scripts.

Largest Contentful Paint

Result: 4.0 s

Why: The main visible content is taking 4.0 s to appear. A slow Largest Contentful Paint usually means the main image, heading, or other large element is loading too late.

How to fix: Optimize the main hero image, use WebP or AVIF, preload the LCP resource when appropriate, reduce render-blocking resources, and defer non-critical JavaScript.

Speed Index

Result: 5.9 s

Why: Visible page content is appearing slowly (5.9 s), so users may see an incomplete page for longer.

How to fix: Optimize above-the-fold images, reduce render-blocking resources, inline critical CSS, and defer non-critical assets.

Use efficient cache lifetimes

Result: Est savings of 136 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 136 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: A long cache lifetime can speed up repeat visits to your page. [Learn more about caching](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cache).

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/logo4-new.png
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/part5.webp
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/part6.webp
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/part4.webp
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/part2.webp

Document request latency

Result: Est savings of 900 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 900 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Your first network request is the most important. [Reduce its latency](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/document-latency) by avoiding redirects, ensuring a fast server response, and enabling text compression.

Font display

Result: Est savings of 150 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 150 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider setting [font-display](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/font-display) to swap or optional to ensure text is consistently visible. swap can be further optimized to mitigate layout shifts with [font metric overrides](https://developer.chrome.com/blog/font-fallbacks).

Affected Elements:

  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-regular-400.woff2
  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-solid-900.woff2

Improve image delivery

Result: Est savings of 405 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 405 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Reducing the download time of images can improve the perceived load time of the page and LCP. [Learn more about optimizing image size](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/image-delivery)

Affected Elements:

  • div#swiper-wrapper-b3d4a62f5ce28559 > div.swiper-slide > div.logo-box > img

LCP breakdown

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Each [subpart has specific improvement strategies](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-breakdown). Ideally, most of the LCP time should be spent on loading the resources, not within delays.

LCP request discovery

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Optimize LCP](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-discovery) by making the LCP image discoverable from the HTML immediately, and avoiding lazy-loading

Network dependency tree

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Avoid chaining critical requests](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/network-dependency-tree) by reducing the length of chains, reducing the download size of resources, or deferring the download of unnecessary resources to improve page load.

Render-blocking requests

Result: Est savings of 990 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 990 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Requests are blocking the page's initial render, which may delay LCP. [Deferring or inlining](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/render-blocking) can move these network requests out of the critical path.

Reduce unused CSS

Result: Est savings of 29 KiB

Why: The page is downloading resources that are not fully used during the initial page load. This increases network and processing work.

How to fix: Remove unused code, split CSS and JavaScript by page or feature, defer non-critical resources, and load assets only when they are needed.

Minimize main-thread work

Result: 4.9 s

Why: Lighthouse reported 4.9 s for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

Total Blocking Time

Result: 1,310 ms

Why: The browser is spending too much time executing long JavaScript tasks (1,310 ms). This delays user interaction.

How to fix: Reduce JavaScript execution, remove unused JavaScript, split large bundles, defer non-critical scripts, and reduce third-party JavaScript.

Cumulative Layout Shift

Result: 0.44

Why: Page elements are moving unexpectedly during loading (0.44). This can make the page difficult to use and can cause accidental clicks.

How to fix: Reserve space for images and advertisements, define width and height for media, avoid inserting content above existing content, and stabilize fonts before rendering.

Speed Index

Result: 6.1 s

Why: Visible page content is appearing slowly (6.1 s), so users may see an incomplete page for longer.

Forced reflow

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: A forced reflow occurs when JavaScript queries geometric properties (such as offsetWidth) after styles have been invalidated by a change to the DOM state. This can result in poor performance. Learn more about [forced reflows](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/forced-reflow) and possible mitigations.

How to fix: These are the largest layout shifts observed on the page. Each table item represents a single layout shift, and shows the element that shifted the most. Below each item are possible root causes that led to the layout shift. Some of these layout shifts may not be included in the CLS metric value due to [windowing](https://web.dev/articles/cls#what_is_cls). [Learn how to improve CLS](https://web.dev/articles/optimize-cls)

Affected Elements:

  • body.wp-singular > section.hero-section
  • section.hero-section > div.goc-container > div.hero-wrapper > div.hero-image

Problems & How to Fix

— 57/100

First Contentful Paint

Result: 2.5 s

Why: The first visible content is taking 2.5 s to appear. This can make the page feel slow when users first open it.

How to fix: Reduce render-blocking CSS and JavaScript, optimize above-the-fold images, preload important resources, and remove unnecessary third-party scripts.

Largest Contentful Paint

Result: 3.6 s

Why: The main visible content is taking 3.6 s to appear. A slow Largest Contentful Paint usually means the main image, heading, or other large element is loading too late.

How to fix: Optimize the main hero image, use WebP or AVIF, preload the LCP resource when appropriate, reduce render-blocking resources, and defer non-critical JavaScript.

Total Blocking Time

Result: 300 ms

Why: The browser is spending too much time executing long JavaScript tasks (300 ms). This delays user interaction.

How to fix: Reduce JavaScript execution, remove unused JavaScript, split large bundles, defer non-critical scripts, and reduce third-party JavaScript.

Cumulative Layout Shift

Result: 0.438

Why: Page elements are moving unexpectedly during loading (0.438). This can make the page difficult to use and can cause accidental clicks.

How to fix: Reserve space for images and advertisements, define width and height for media, avoid inserting content above existing content, and stabilize fonts before rendering.

Speed Index

Result: 5.1 s

Why: Visible page content is appearing slowly (5.1 s), so users may see an incomplete page for longer.

How to fix: Optimize above-the-fold images, reduce render-blocking resources, inline critical CSS, and defer non-critical assets.

Use efficient cache lifetimes

Result: Est savings of 69 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 69 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: A long cache lifetime can speed up repeat visits to your page. [Learn more about caching](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cache).

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/crm.png
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Operational-delays-can-reduce-productivity.webp
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/themes/getoncrm/assets/js/vendor/swiper-bundle.min.js?ver=11.0.0
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Automotive-and-Component-Manufacturing.webp
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Industrial-Distribution.webp

Layout shift culprits

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Layout shifts occur when elements move absent any user interaction. [Investigate the causes of layout shifts](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cls-culprit), such as elements being added, removed, or their fonts changing as the page loads.

Document request latency

Result: Est savings of 1,100 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 1,100 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Your first network request is the most important. [Reduce its latency](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/document-latency) by avoiding redirects, ensuring a fast server response, and enabling text compression.

Font display

Result: Est savings of 230 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 230 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider setting [font-display](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/font-display) to swap or optional to ensure text is consistently visible. swap can be further optimized to mitigate layout shifts with [font metric overrides](https://developer.chrome.com/blog/font-fallbacks).

Affected Elements:

  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-solid-900.woff2
  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-regular-400.woff2

Forced reflow

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: A forced reflow occurs when JavaScript queries geometric properties (such as offsetWidth) after styles have been invalidated by a change to the DOM state. This can result in poor performance. Learn more about [forced reflows](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/forced-reflow) and possible mitigations.

Improve image delivery

Result: Est savings of 38 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 38 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Reducing the download time of images can improve the perceived load time of the page and LCP. [Learn more about optimizing image size](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/image-delivery)

Affected Elements:

  • div.goc-container > div.timeline > div.timeline-item > div.circle
  • div.goc-container > div.hero-wrapper > div.hero-image > img
  • div.goc-container > div.timeline > div.timeline-item > div.circle
  • div.goc-container > div.timeline > div.timeline-item > div.circle
  • div.goc-container > div.timeline > div.timeline-item > div.circle

LCP breakdown

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Each [subpart has specific improvement strategies](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-breakdown). Ideally, most of the LCP time should be spent on loading the resources, not within delays.

LCP request discovery

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Optimize LCP](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-discovery) by making the LCP image discoverable from the HTML immediately, and avoiding lazy-loading

Network dependency tree

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Avoid chaining critical requests](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/network-dependency-tree) by reducing the length of chains, reducing the download size of resources, or deferring the download of unnecessary resources to improve page load.

Render-blocking requests

Result: Est savings of 400 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 400 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Requests are blocking the page's initial render, which may delay LCP. [Deferring or inlining](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/render-blocking) can move these network requests out of the critical path.

Time to Interactive

Result: 6.0 s

Why: Lighthouse reported 6.0 s for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Time to Interactive is the amount of time it takes for the page to become fully interactive. [Learn more about the Time to Interactive metric](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/interactive/).

Max Potential First Input Delay

Result: 380 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported 380 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: The maximum potential First Input Delay that your users could experience is the duration of the longest task. [Learn more about the Maximum Potential First Input Delay metric](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/lighthouse-max-potential-fid/).

Minify CSS

Result: Est savings of 5 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 5 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Minifying CSS files can reduce network payload sizes. [Learn how to minify CSS](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/unminified-css/).

Reduce unused CSS

Result: Est savings of 29 KiB

Why: The page is downloading resources that are not fully used during the initial page load. This increases network and processing work.

How to fix: Remove unused code, split CSS and JavaScript by page or feature, defer non-critical resources, and load assets only when they are needed.

Minimize main-thread work

Result: 6.6 s

Why: Lighthouse reported 6.6 s for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider reducing the time spent parsing, compiling and executing JS. You may find delivering smaller JS payloads helps with this. [Learn how to minimize main-thread work](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/mainthread-work-breakdown/)

Image elements do not have explicit `width` and `height`

How to fix: The maximum potential First Input Delay that your users could experience is the duration of the longest task. [Learn more about the Maximum Potential First Input Delay metric](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/lighthouse-max-potential-fid/).

Minify CSS

Result: Est savings of 5 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 5 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Minifying CSS files can reduce network payload sizes. [Learn how to minify CSS](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/unminified-css/).

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/themes/getoncrm/assets/css/style.css?ver=1786701773

Reduce unused CSS

Result: Est savings of 29 KiB

Why: The page is downloading resources that are not fully used during the initial page load. This increases network and processing work.

How to fix: Remove unused code, split CSS and JavaScript by page or feature, defer non-critical resources, and load assets only when they are needed.

Affected Elements:

  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/css/all.min.css?ver=6.7.2
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/themes/getoncrm/assets/css/vendor/bootstrap.min.css?ver=5.3.3

Avoid enormous network payloads

Result: Total size was 14,625 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Total size was 14,625 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Large network payloads cost users real money and are highly correlated with long load times. [Learn how to reduce payload sizes](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/total-byte-weight/).

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/themes/getoncrm/assets/image/robotic-welding.mp4
  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-solid-900.woff2

  • https://fonts.gstatic.com/s/inter/v20/UcCo3FwrK3iLTcviYwYZ8UA3.woff2

Minimize main-thread work

Result: 7.9 s

Why: Lighthouse reported 7.9 s for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider reducing the time spent parsing, compiling and executing JS. You may find delivering smaller JS payloads helps with this. [Learn how to minimize main-thread work](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/mainthread-work-breakdown/)

Image elements do not have explicit `width` and `height`

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

Affected Elements:

  • li.nav-item > button.nav-link > span > img

Result: Est savings of 1,020 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 1,020 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Redirects introduce additional delays before the page can be loaded. [Learn how to avoid page redirects](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/redirects/).

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/revenue-cloud-for-manufacturing/

Problems & How to Fix

— 69/100

First Contentful Paint

Result: 2.6 s

Why: The first visible content is taking 2.6 s to appear. This can make the page feel slow when users first open it.

How to fix: Reduce render-blocking CSS and JavaScript, optimize above-the-fold images, preload important resources, and remove unnecessary third-party scripts.

Largest Contentful Paint

Result: 3.0 s

Why: The main visible content is taking 3.0 s to appear. A slow Largest Contentful Paint usually means the main image, heading, or other large element is loading too late.

How to fix: Optimize the main hero image, use WebP or AVIF, preload the LCP resource when appropriate, reduce render-blocking resources, and defer non-critical JavaScript.

Cumulative Layout Shift

Result: 0.41

Why: Page elements are moving unexpectedly during loading (0.41). This can make the page difficult to use and can cause accidental clicks.

How to fix: Reserve space for images and advertisements, define width and height for media, avoid inserting content above existing content, and stabilize fonts before rendering.

Speed Index

Result: 4.6 s

Why: Visible page content is appearing slowly (4.6 s), so users may see an incomplete page for longer.

How to fix: Optimize above-the-fold images, reduce render-blocking resources, inline critical CSS, and defer non-critical assets.

Use efficient cache lifetimes

Result: Est savings of 54 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 54 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: A long cache lifetime can speed up repeat visits to your page. [Learn more about caching](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cache).

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Seasonal-demand-changes-rapidly.webp
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/themes/getoncrm/assets/js/vendor/swiper-bundle.min.js?ver=11.0.0
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/efficiency.png
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/billing.png
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/automation.png

Layout shift culprits

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Layout shifts occur when elements move absent any user interaction. [Investigate the causes of layout shifts](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cls-culprit), such as elements being added, removed, or their fonts changing as the page loads.

Document request latency

Result: Est savings of 380 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 380 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Your first network request is the most important. [Reduce its latency](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/document-latency) by avoiding redirects, ensuring a fast server response, and enabling text compression.

Font display

Result: Est savings of 130 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 130 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider setting [font-display](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/font-display) to swap or optional to ensure text is consistently visible. swap can be further optimized to mitigate layout shifts with [font metric overrides](https://developer.chrome.com/blog/font-fallbacks).

Affected Elements:

  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-solid-900.woff2
  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-regular-400.woff2

Improve image delivery

Result: Est savings of 50 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 50 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Reducing the download time of images can improve the perceived load time of the page and LCP. [Learn more about optimizing image size](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/image-delivery)

Affected Elements:

  • div.card > div.circle-area > div.icon > img
  • div.card > div.circle-area > div.icon > img

LCP request discovery

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Optimize LCP](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-discovery) by making the LCP image discoverable from the HTML immediately, and avoiding lazy-loading

Network dependency tree

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Avoid chaining critical requests](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/network-dependency-tree) by reducing the length of chains, reducing the download size of resources, or deferring the download of unnecessary resources to improve page load.

Render-blocking requests

Result: Est savings of 920 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 920 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Requests are blocking the page's initial render, which may delay LCP. [Deferring or inlining](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/render-blocking) can move these network requests out of the critical path.

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 31 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Reducing the download time of images can improve the perceived load time of the page and LCP. [Learn more about optimizing image size](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/image-delivery)

LCP breakdown

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Each [subpart has specific improvement strategies](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-breakdown). Ideally, most of the LCP time should be spent on loading the resources, not within delays.

LCP request discovery

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Optimize LCP](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-discovery) by making the LCP image discoverable from the HTML immediately, and avoiding lazy-loading

Network dependency tree

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Avoid chaining critical requests](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/network-dependency-tree) by reducing the length of chains, reducing the download size of resources, or deferring the download of unnecessary resources to improve page load.

Render-blocking requests

Result: Est savings of 650 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 650 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Requests are blocking the page's initial render, which may delay LCP. [Deferring or inlining](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/render-blocking) can move these network requests out of the critical path.

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-includes/js/jquery/jquery-migrate.min.js?ver=3.4.1

Time to Interactive

Result: 6.1 s

Problems & How to Fix

— 68/100

First Contentful Paint

Result: 2.6 s

Why: The first visible content is taking 2.6 s to appear. This can make the page feel slow when users first open it.

How to fix: Reduce render-blocking CSS and JavaScript, optimize above-the-fold images, preload important resources, and remove unnecessary third-party scripts.

Largest Contentful Paint

Result: 3.0 s

Why: The main visible content is taking 3.0 s to appear. A slow Largest Contentful Paint usually means the main image, heading, or other large element is loading too late.

How to fix: Optimize the main hero image, use WebP or AVIF, preload the LCP resource when appropriate, reduce render-blocking resources, and defer non-critical JavaScript.

Cumulative Layout Shift

Result: 0.41

Why: Page elements are moving unexpectedly during loading (0.41). This can make the page difficult to use and can cause accidental clicks.

How to fix: Reserve space for images and advertisements, define width and height for media, avoid inserting content above existing content, and stabilize fonts before rendering.

Speed Index

Result: 5.1 s

Why: Visible page content is appearing slowly (5.1 s), so users may see an incomplete page for longer.

How to fix: Optimize above-the-fold images, reduce render-blocking resources, inline critical CSS, and defer non-critical assets.

Use efficient cache lifetimes

Result: Est savings of 50 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 50 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: A long cache lifetime can speed up repeat visits to your page. [Learn more about caching](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cache).

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Manual-Work-Slows-Tech-Growth.webp

Layout shift culprits

How to fix: Layout shifts occur when elements move absent any user interaction. [Investigate the causes of layout shifts](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cls-culprit), such as elements being added, removed, or their fonts changing as the page loads.

Document request latency

Result: Est savings of 910 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 910 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Your first network request is the most important. [Reduce its latency](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/document-latency) by avoiding redirects, ensuring a fast server response, and enabling text compression.

Font display

Result: Est savings of 210 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 210 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider setting [font-display](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/font-display) to swap or optional to ensure text is consistently visible. swap can be further optimized to mitigate layout shifts with [font metric overrides](https://developer.chrome.com/blog/font-fallbacks).

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Disconnected-Revenue-Processes-Slow-Growth.-Agentforce-Unifies-Every-Revenue-Step.webp
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/themes/getoncrm/assets/js/vendor/swiper-bundle.min.js?ver=11.0.0

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/billing.png

Layout shift culprits

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Layout shifts occur when elements move absent any user interaction. [Investigate the causes of layout shifts](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cls-culprit), such as elements being added, removed, or their fonts changing as the page loads.

Document request latency

Result: Est savings of 1,150 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 1,150 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Your first network request is the most important. [Reduce its latency](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/document-latency) by avoiding redirects, ensuring a fast server response, and enabling text compression.

Font display

Result: Est savings of 140 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 140 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider setting [font-display](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/font-display) to swap or optional to ensure text is consistently visible. swap can be further optimized to mitigate layout shifts with [font metric overrides](https://developer.chrome.com/blog/font-fallbacks).

Affected Elements:

  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-regular-400.woff2
  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-solid-900.woff2

Improve image delivery

Result: Est savings of 32 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 32 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Reducing the download time of images can improve the perceived load time of the page and LCP. [Learn more about optimizing image size](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/image-delivery)

Affected Elements:

  • div.card > div.circle-area > div.icon > img

LCP breakdown

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Each [subpart has specific improvement strategies](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-breakdown). Ideally, most of the LCP time should be spent on loading the resources, not within delays.

LCP request discovery

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Optimize LCP](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-discovery) by making the LCP image discoverable from the HTML immediately, and avoiding lazy-loading

Network dependency tree

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Avoid chaining critical requests](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/network-dependency-tree) by reducing the length of chains, reducing the download size of resources, or deferring the download of unnecessary resources to improve page load.

Render-blocking requests

Result: Est savings of 900 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 900 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Requests are blocking the page's initial render, which may delay LCP. [Deferring or inlining](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/render-blocking) can move these network requests out of the critical path.

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-includes/js/jquery/jquery-migrate.min.js?ver=3.4.1
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-includes/js/jquery/jquery.min.js?ver=3.7.1
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/themes/getoncrm/style.css?ver=1.0.0

Time to Interactive

Result: 6.5 s

Why: Lighthouse reported 6.5 s for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Time to Interactive is the amount of time it takes for the page to become fully interactive. [Learn more about the Time to Interactive metric](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/interactive/).

Minify CSS

Result: Est savings of 5 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 5 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Minifying CSS files can reduce network payload sizes. [Learn how to minify CSS](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/unminified-css/).

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/themes/getoncrm/assets/css/style.css?ver=1786701773

Reduce unused CSS

Result: Est savings of 30 KiB

Why: The page is downloading resources that are not fully used during the initial page load. This increases network and processing work.

How to fix: Remove unused code, split CSS and JavaScript by page or feature, defer non-critical resources, and load assets only when they are needed.

Affected Elements:

  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/css/all.min.css?ver=6.7.2

Minimize main-thread work

Result: 7.5 s

Why: Lighthouse reported 7.5 s for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider reducing the time spent parsing, compiling and executing JS. You may find delivering smaller JS payloads helps with this. [Learn how to minimize main-thread work](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/mainthread-work-breakdown/)

Image elements do not have explicit `width` and `height`

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Set an explicit width and height on image elements to reduce layout shifts and improve CLS. [Learn how to set image dimensions](https://web.dev/articles/optimize-cls#images_without_dimensions)

Result: Est savings of 1,020 ms

Result: Root document took 840 ms

How to fix: Keep the server response time for the main document short because all other requests depend on it. [Learn more about the Time to First Byte metric](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/time-to-first-byte/).

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/agentforce-revenue-management/

Problems & How to Fix

— 69/100

First Contentful Paint

Result: 2.7 s

Why: The first visible content is taking 2.7 s to appear. This can make the page feel slow when users first open it.

How to fix: Reduce render-blocking CSS and JavaScript, optimize above-the-fold images, preload important resources, and remove unnecessary third-party scripts.

Largest Contentful Paint

Result: 2.9 s

Why: The main visible content is taking 2.9 s to appear. A slow Largest Contentful Paint usually means the main image, heading, or other large element is loading too late.

How to fix: Optimize the main hero image, use WebP or AVIF, preload the LCP resource when appropriate, reduce render-blocking resources, and defer non-critical JavaScript.

Cumulative Layout Shift

Result: 0.435

Why: Page elements are moving unexpectedly during loading (0.435). This can make the page difficult to use and can cause accidental clicks.

How to fix: Reserve space for images and advertisements, define width and height for media, avoid inserting content above existing content, and stabilize fonts before rendering.

Speed Index

Result: 4.5 s

Why: Visible page content is appearing slowly (4.5 s), so users may see an incomplete page for longer.

How to fix: Optimize above-the-fold images, reduce render-blocking resources, inline critical CSS, and defer non-critical assets.

Use efficient cache lifetimes

Result: Est savings of 46 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 46 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: A long cache lifetime can speed up repeat visits to your page. [Learn more about caching](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cache).

First Contentful Paint

Result: 2.6 s

Why: The first visible content is taking 2.6 s to appear. This can make the page feel slow when users first open it.

How to fix: Reduce render-blocking CSS and JavaScript, optimize above-the-fold images, preload important resources, and remove unnecessary third-party scripts.

Largest Contentful Paint

Result: 3.2 s

Why: The main visible content is taking 3.2 s to appear. A slow Largest Contentful Paint usually means the main image, heading, or other large element is loading too late.

How to fix: Optimize the main hero image, use WebP or AVIF, preload the LCP resource when appropriate, reduce render-blocking resources, and defer non-critical JavaScript.

Cumulative Layout Shift

Result: 0.409

Why: Page elements are moving unexpectedly during loading (0.409). This can make the page difficult to use and can cause accidental clicks.

How to fix: Reserve space for images and advertisements, define width and height for media, avoid inserting content above existing content, and stabilize fonts before rendering.

Speed Index

Result: 5.0 s

Why: Visible page content is appearing slowly (5.0 s), so users may see an incomplete page for longer.

How to fix: Optimize above-the-fold images, reduce render-blocking resources, inline critical CSS, and defer non-critical assets.

Use efficient cache lifetimes

Result: Est savings of 56 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 56 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: A long cache lifetime can speed up repeat visits to your page. [Learn more about caching](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cache).

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Stop-losing-time-between-quote-and-invoice-1.webp
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/themes/getoncrm/assets/js/vendor/swiper-bundle.min.js?ver=11.0.0
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/efficiency.png
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/automation.png
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-includes/js/jquery/jquery.min.js?ver=3.7.1

Layout shift culprits

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Layout shifts occur when elements move absent any user interaction. [Investigate the causes of layout shifts](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cls-culprit), such as elements being added, removed, or their fonts changing as the page loads.

Document request latency

Result: Est savings of 1,170 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 1,170 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Your first network request is the most important. [Reduce its latency](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/document-latency) by avoiding redirects, ensuring a fast server response, and enabling text compression.

Font display

Result: Est savings of 160 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 160 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider setting [font-display](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/font-display) to swap or optional to ensure text is consistently visible. swap can be further optimized to mitigate layout shifts with [font metric overrides](https://developer.chrome.com/blog/font-fallbacks).

Affected Elements:

  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-regular-400.woff2
  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-solid-900.woff2

Improve image delivery

Result: Est savings of 36 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 36 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Reducing the download time of images can improve the perceived load time of the page and LCP. [Learn more about optimizing image size](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/image-delivery)

Affected Elements:

  • div.goc-container > div.hero-wrapper > div.hero-image > img

LCP breakdown

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Each [subpart has specific improvement strategies](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-breakdown). Ideally, most of the LCP time should be spent on loading the resources, not within delays.

LCP request discovery

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Optimize LCP](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-discovery) by making the LCP image discoverable from the HTML immediately, and avoiding lazy-loading

Network dependency tree

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Avoid chaining critical requests](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/network-dependency-tree) by reducing the length of chains, reducing the download size of resources, or deferring the download of unnecessary resources to improve page load.

Render-blocking requests

Result: Est savings of 690 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 690 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Requests are blocking the page's initial render, which may delay LCP. [Deferring or inlining](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/render-blocking) can move these network requests out of the critical path.

Reduce unused CSS

Result: Est savings of 30 KiB

Why: The page is downloading resources that are not fully used during the initial page load. This increases network and processing work.

How to fix: Remove unused code, split CSS and JavaScript by page or feature, defer non-critical resources, and load assets only when they are needed.

Minimize main-thread work

Result: 6.3 s

Why: Lighthouse reported 6.3 s for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider reducing the time spent parsing, compiling and executing JS. You may find delivering smaller JS payloads helps with this. [Learn how to minimize main-thread work](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/mainthread-work-breakdown/)

First Contentful Paint

Result: 2.7 s

Why: The first visible content is taking 2.7 s to appear. This can make the page feel slow when users first open it.

Largest Contentful Paint

Result: 3.9 s

Why: The main visible content is taking 3.9 s to appear. A slow Largest Contentful Paint usually means the main image, heading, or other large element is loading too late.

How to fix: Optimize the main hero image, use WebP or AVIF, preload the LCP resource when appropriate, reduce render-blocking resources, and defer non-critical JavaScript.

Cumulative Layout Shift

Result: 0.425

Why: Page elements are moving unexpectedly during loading (0.425). This can make the page difficult to use and can cause accidental clicks.

How to fix: Reserve space for images and advertisements, define width and height for media, avoid inserting content above existing content, and stabilize fonts before rendering.

Speed Index

Result: 5.0 s

Why: Visible page content is appearing slowly (5.0 s), so users may see an incomplete page for longer.

How to fix: Optimize above-the-fold images, reduce render-blocking resources, inline critical CSS, and defer non-critical assets.

How to fix: Your first network request is the most important. [Reduce its latency](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/document-latency) by avoiding redirects, ensuring a fast server response, and enabling text compression.

Font display

Result: Est savings of 230 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 230 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider setting [font-display](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/font-display) to swap or optional to ensure text is consistently visible. swap can be further optimized to mitigate layout shifts with [font metric overrides](https://developer.chrome.com/blog/font-fallbacks).

Affected Elements:

  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-solid-900.woff2
  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-regular-400.woff2

Forced reflow

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: A forced reflow occurs when JavaScript queries geometric properties (such as offsetWidth) after styles have been invalidated by a change to the DOM state. This can result in poor performance. Learn more about [forced reflows](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/forced-reflow) and possible mitigations.

Performance — 59/100

Page: https://goc.searchonic.online/agentforce-for-medical-devices

First Contentful Paint

Result: 2.6 s

Why: The first visible content is taking 2.6 s to appear. This can make the page feel slow when users first open it.

How to fix: Reduce render-blocking CSS and JavaScript, optimize above-the-fold images, preload important resources, and remove unnecessary third-party scripts.

Largest Contentful Paint

Result: 4.5 s

Why: The main visible content is taking 4.5 s to appear. A slow Largest Contentful Paint usually means the main image, heading, or other large element is loading too late.

How to fix: Optimize the main hero image, use WebP or AVIF, preload the LCP resource when appropriate, reduce render-blocking resources, and defer non-critical JavaScript.

Cumulative Layout Shift

Result: 0.409

Why: Page elements are moving unexpectedly during loading (0.409). This can make the page difficult to use and can cause accidental clicks.

How to fix: Reserve space for images and advertisements, define width and height for media, avoid inserting content above existing content, and stabilize fonts before rendering.

Speed Index

Result: 4.5 s

Why: Visible page content is appearing slowly (4.5 s), so users may see an incomplete page for longer.

How to fix: Optimize above-the-fold images, reduce render-blocking resources, inline critical CSS, and defer non-critical assets.

Result: Root document took 640 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Root document took 640 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/revenue-cloud-for-manufacturing/

Result: 3 layout shifts found

Performance — 69/100

Page: https://goc.searchonic.online/revenue-cloud-for-food-beverage

First Contentful Paint

Result: 2.6 s

Why: The first visible content is taking 2.6 s to appear. This can make the page feel slow when users first open it.

How to fix: Reduce render-blocking CSS and JavaScript, optimize above-the-fold images, preload important resources, and remove unnecessary third-party scripts.

Largest Contentful Paint

Result: 3.0 s

Why: The main visible content is taking 3.0 s to appear. A slow Largest Contentful Paint usually means the main image, heading, or other large element is loading too late.

How to fix: Optimize the main hero image, use WebP or AVIF, preload the LCP resource when appropriate, reduce render-blocking resources, and defer non-critical JavaScript.

Cumulative Layout Shift

Result: 0.41

Why: Page elements are moving unexpectedly during loading (0.41). This can make the page difficult to use and can cause accidental clicks.

How to fix: Reserve space for images and advertisements, define width and height for media, avoid inserting content above existing content, and stabilize fonts before rendering.

Speed Index

Result: 4.6 s

Why: Visible page content is appearing slowly (4.6 s), so users may see an incomplete page for longer.

How to fix: Optimize above-the-fold images, reduce render-blocking resources, inline critical CSS, and defer non-critical assets.

Use efficient cache lifetimes

Result: Est savings of 54 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 54 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: A long cache lifetime can speed up repeat visits to your page. [Learn more about caching](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cache).

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Seasonal-demand-changes-rapidly.webp
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/themes/getoncrm/assets/js/vendor/swiper-bundle.min.js?ver=11.0.0
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/efficiency.png
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/billing.png
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/automation.png

Layout shift culprits

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Layout shifts occur when elements move absent any user interaction. [Investigate the causes of layout shifts](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cls-culprit), such as elements being added, removed, or their fonts changing as the page loads.

Document request latency

Result: Est savings of 380 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 380 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Your first network request is the most important. [Reduce its latency](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/document-latency) by avoiding redirects, ensuring a fast server response, and enabling text compression.

Font display

Result: Est savings of 130 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 130 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider setting [font-display](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/font-display) to swap or optional to ensure text is consistently visible. swap can be further optimized to mitigate layout shifts with [font metric overrides](https://developer.chrome.com/blog/font-fallbacks).

Affected Elements:

  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-solid-900.woff2
  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-regular-400.woff2

Improve image delivery

Result: Est savings of 50 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 50 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Reducing the download time of images can improve the perceived load time of the page and LCP. [Learn more about optimizing image size](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/image-delivery)

Affected Elements:

  • div.card > div.circle-area > div.icon > img
  • div.card > div.circle-area > div.icon > img

LCP request discovery

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Optimize LCP](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-discovery) by making the LCP image discoverable from the HTML immediately, and avoiding lazy-loading

Network dependency tree

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Avoid chaining critical requests](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/network-dependency-tree) by reducing the length of chains, reducing the download size of resources, or deferring the download of unnecessary resources to improve page load.

Render-blocking requests

Result: Est savings of 920 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 920 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Requests are blocking the page's initial render, which may delay LCP. [Deferring or inlining](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/render-blocking) can move these network requests out of the critical path.

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-includes/js/jquery/jquery.min.js?ver=3.7.1
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-includes/js/jquery/jquery-migrate.min.js?ver=3.4.1

How to fix: Remove unused code, split CSS and JavaScript by page or feature, defer non-critical resources, and load assets only when they are needed.

How to fix: Consider reducing the time spent parsing, compiling and executing JS. You may find delivering smaller JS payloads helps with this. [Learn how to minimize main-thread work](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/mainthread-work-breakdown/)

How to fix: Set an explicit width and height on image elements to reduce layout shifts and improve CLS. [Learn how to set image dimensions](https://web.dev/articles/optimize-cls#images_without_dimensions)

Performance — 68/100

Page: https://goc.searchonic.online/agentforce-for-martech

First Contentful Paint

Result: 2.6 s

Why: The first visible content is taking 2.6 s to appear. This can make the page feel slow when users first open it.

How to fix: Reduce render-blocking CSS and JavaScript, optimize above-the-fold images, preload important resources, and remove unnecessary third-party scripts.

Largest Contentful Paint

Result: 3.0 s

Why: The main visible content is taking 3.0 s to appear. A slow Largest Contentful Paint usually means the main image, heading, or other large element is loading too late.

How to fix: Optimize the main hero image, use WebP or AVIF, preload the LCP resource when appropriate, reduce render-blocking resources, and defer non-critical JavaScript.

Cumulative Layout Shift

Result: 0.41

Why: Page elements are moving unexpectedly during loading (0.41). This can make the page difficult to use and can cause accidental clicks.

How to fix: Reserve space for images and advertisements, define width and height for media, avoid inserting content above existing content, and stabilize fonts before rendering.

Speed Index

Result: 5.1 s

Why: Visible page content is appearing slowly (5.1 s), so users may see an incomplete page for longer.

How to fix: Optimize above-the-fold images, reduce render-blocking resources, inline critical CSS, and defer non-critical assets.

Use efficient cache lifetimes

Result: Est savings of 50 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 50 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: A long cache lifetime can speed up repeat visits to your page. [Learn more about caching](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cache).

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Manual-Work-Slows-Tech-Growth.webp
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/themes/getoncrm/assets/js/vendor/swiper-bundle.min.js?ver=11.0.0
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-includes/js/jquery/jquery.min.js?ver=3.7.1
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Technology-Startups-Scale-Ups.webp
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/themes/getoncrm/assets/css/vendor/bootstrap.min.css?ver=5.3.3

Layout shift culprits

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Layout shifts occur when elements move absent any user interaction. [Investigate the causes of layout shifts](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cls-culprit), such as elements being added, removed, or their fonts changing as the page loads.

Document request latency

Result: Est savings of 910 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 910 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Your first network request is the most important. [Reduce its latency](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/document-latency) by avoiding redirects, ensuring a fast server response, and enabling text compression.

Font display

Result: Est savings of 210 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 210 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider setting [font-display](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/font-display) to swap or optional to ensure text is consistently visible. swap can be further optimized to mitigate layout shifts with [font metric overrides](https://developer.chrome.com/blog/font-fallbacks).

Affected Elements:

  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-regular-400.woff2
  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-solid-900.woff2

LCP breakdown

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Each [subpart has specific improvement strategies](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-breakdown). Ideally, most of the LCP time should be spent on loading the resources, not within delays.

LCP request discovery

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Optimize LCP](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-discovery) by making the LCP image discoverable from the HTML immediately, and avoiding lazy-loading

Network dependency tree

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Avoid chaining critical requests](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/network-dependency-tree) by reducing the length of chains, reducing the download size of resources, or deferring the download of unnecessary resources to improve page load.

Render-blocking requests

Result: Est savings of 520 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 520 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Requests are blocking the page's initial render, which may delay LCP. [Deferring or inlining](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/render-blocking) can move these network requests out of the critical path.

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-includes/js/jquery/jquery-migrate.min.js?ver=3.4.1
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-includes/js/jquery/jquery.min.js?ver=3.7.1

Result: 4.6 s

How to fix: Time to Interactive is the amount of time it takes for the page to become fully interactive. [Learn more about the Time to Interactive metric](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/interactive/).

Minify CSS

Result: Est savings of 5 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 5 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

Reduce unused CSS

Result: Est savings of 18 KiB

How to fix: Remove unused code, split CSS and JavaScript by page or feature, defer non-critical resources, and load assets only when they are needed.

  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/css/all.min.css?ver=6.7.2
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/themes/getoncrm/assets/css/vendor/bootstrap.min.css?ver=5.3.3

Minimize main-thread work

Result: 7.5 s

Why: Lighthouse reported 7.5 s for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider reducing the time spent parsing, compiling and executing JS. You may find delivering smaller JS payloads helps with this. [Learn how to minimize main-thread work](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/mainthread-work-breakdown/)

Image elements do not have explicit `width` and `height`

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Set an explicit width and height on image elements to reduce layout shifts and improve CLS. [Learn how to set image dimensions](https://web.dev/articles/optimize-cls#images_without_dimensions)

Affected Elements:

  • div.goc-container > div.hero-wrapper > div.hero-image > img
  • div.card > div.circle-area > div.icon > img
  • div.usecase-item > div.usecase-box > span.ins-icon > img
  • div.usecase-item > div.usecase-box > span.ins-icon > img
  • div.usecase-item > div.usecase-box > span.ins-icon > img

Avoid multiple page redirects

Result: Est savings of 1,020 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 1,020 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Redirects introduce additional delays before the page can be loaded. [Learn how to avoid page redirects](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/redirects/).

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/agentforce-revenue-management
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/agentforce-revenue-management/

Reduce initial server response time

Result: Root document took 840 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Root document took 840 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Keep the server response time for the main document short because all other requests depend on it. [Learn more about the Time to First Byte metric](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/time-to-first-byte/).

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/agentforce-revenue-management/

Avoid large layout shifts

Result: 2 layout shifts found

How to fix: These are the largest layout shifts observed on the page. Each table item represents a single layout shift, and shows the element that shifted the most. Performance — 69/100

Page: https://goc.searchonic.online/subscription-billing-management

First Contentful Paint

Result: 2.7 s

Why: The first visible content is taking 2.7 s to appear. This can make the page feel slow when users first open it.

How to fix: Reduce render-blocking CSS and JavaScript, optimize above-the-fold images, preload important resources, and remove unnecessary third-party scripts.

Largest Contentful Paint

Result: 2.9 s

Why: The main visible content is taking 2.9 s to appear. A slow Largest Contentful Paint usually means the main image, heading, or other large element is loading too late.

How to fix: Optimize the main hero image, use WebP or AVIF, preload the LCP resource when appropriate, reduce render-blocking resources, and defer non-critical JavaScript.

Cumulative Layout Shift

Result: 0.435

Why: Page elements are moving unexpectedly during loading (0.435). This can make the page difficult to use and can cause accidental clicks.

How to fix: Reserve space for images and advertisements, define width and height for media, avoid inserting content above existing content, and stabilize fonts before rendering.

Speed Index

Result: 4.5 s

Why: Visible page content is appearing slowly (4.5 s), so users may see an incomplete page for longer.

How to fix: Optimize above-the-fold images, reduce render-blocking resources, inline critical CSS, and defer non-critical assets.

Use efficient cache lifetimes

Result: Est savings of 46 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 46 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: A long cache lifetime can speed up repeat visits to your page. [Learn more about caching](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cache).

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/themes/getoncrm/assets/js/vendor/swiper-bundle.min.js?ver=11.0.0
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Turn-every-subscription-cycle-into-predictable-revenue.webp
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/automation.png
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-includes/js/jquery/jquery.min.js?ver=3.7.1
  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/inflation-rate.png

Layout shift culprits

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Layout shifts occur when elements move absent any user interaction. [Investigate the causes of layout shifts](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cls-culprit), such as elements being added, removed, or their fonts changing as the page loads.

Document request latency

Result: Est savings of 1,150 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 1,150 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Your first network request is the most important. [Reduce its latency](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/document-latency) by avoiding redirects, ensuring a fast server response, and enabling text compression.

Font display

Result: Est savings of 210 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 210 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider setting [font-display](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/font-display) to swap or optional to ensure text is consistently visible. swap can be further optimized to mitigate layout shifts with [font metric overrides](https://developer.chrome.com/blog/font-fallbacks).

Affected Elements:

  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-solid-900.woff2
  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-regular-400.woff2

Forced reflow

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: A forced reflow occurs when JavaScript queries geometric properties (such as offsetWidth) after styles have been invalidated by a change to the DOM state. This can result in poor performance. Learn more about [forced reflows](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/forced-reflow) and possible mitigations.

Improve image delivery

Result: Est savings of 69 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 69 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Reducing the download time of images can improve the perceived load time of the page and LCP. [Learn more about optimizing image size](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/image-delivery)

Affected Elements:

  • div.usecase-item > div.usecase-box > span.ins-icon > img
  • div.usecase-item > div.usecase-box > span.ins-icon > img
  • div.usecase-item > div.usecase-box > span.ins-icon > img

LCP breakdown

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Each [subpart has specific improvement strategies](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-breakdown). Ideally, most of the LCP time should be spent on loading the resources, not within delays.

LCP request discovery

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Optimize LCP](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/lcp-discovery) by making the LCP image discoverable from the HTML immediately, and avoiding lazy-loading

Network dependency tree

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: [Avoid chaining critical requests](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/network-dependency-tree) by reducing the length of chains, reducing the download size of resources, or deferring the download of unnecessary resources to improve page load.

Render-blocking requests

Result: Est savings of 1,000 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 1,000 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Requests are blocking the page's initial render, which may delay LCP. [Deferring or inlining](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/render-blocking) can move these network requests out of the critical path.

Affected Elements:

Performance — 60/100

Page: https://goc.searchonic.online/revenue-recognition-automation

First Contentful Paint

Result: 2.7 s

Why: The first visible content is taking 2.7 s to appear. This can make the page feel slow when users first open it.

How to fix: Reduce render-blocking CSS and JavaScript, optimize above-the-fold images, preload important resources, and remove unnecessary third-party scripts.

Largest Contentful Paint

Result: 4.3 s

Why: The main visible content is taking 4.3 s to appear. A slow Largest Contentful Paint usually means the main image, heading, or other large element is loading too late.

How to fix: Optimize the main hero image, use WebP or AVIF, preload the LCP resource when appropriate, reduce render-blocking resources, and defer non-critical JavaScript.

Cumulative Layout Shift

Result: 0.409

Why: Page elements are moving unexpectedly during loading (0.409). This can make the page difficult to use and can cause accidental clicks.

How to fix: Reserve space for images and advertisements, define width and height for media, avoid inserting content above existing content, and stabilize fonts before rendering.

Speed Index

Result: 4.5 s

Why: Visible page content is appearing slowly (4.5 s), so users may see an incomplete page for longer.

How to fix: Optimize above-the-fold images, reduce render-blocking resources, inline critical CSS, and defer non-critical assets.

Use efficient cache lifetimes

Result: Est savings of 57 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 57 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: A long cache lifetime can speed up repeat visits to your page. [Learn more about caching](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cache).

Affected Elements:

  • https://goc.searchonic.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/saas-and-subscription.svg

Why: Lighthouse identified an issue with this page.

How to fix: Layout shifts occur when elements move absent any user interaction. [Investigate the causes of layout shifts](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/cls-culprit), such as elements being added, removed, or their fonts changing as the page loads.

Document request latency

Result: Est savings of 1,050 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 1,050 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Your first network request is the most important. [Reduce its latency](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/document-latency) by avoiding redirects, ensuring a fast server response, and enabling text compression.

Font display

Result: Est savings of 160 ms

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 160 ms for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Consider setting [font-display](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/font-display) to swap or optional to ensure text is consistently visible. swap can be further optimized to mitigate layout shifts with [font metric overrides](https://developer.chrome.com/blog/font-fallbacks).

Affected Elements:

  • https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/webfonts/fa-regular-400.woff2

Result: Est savings of 17 KiB

Why: Lighthouse reported Est savings of 17 KiB for this audit, indicating that this check did not meet the recommended threshold.

How to fix: Reducing the download time of images can improve the perceived load time of the page and LCP. [Learn more about optimizing image size](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/performance/insights/image-delivery)

First Contentful Paint

Result: 2.7 s

Why: The first visible content is taking 2.7 s to appear. This can make the page feel slow when users first open it.

How to fix: Reduce render-blocking CSS and JavaScript, optimize above-the-fold images, preload important resources, and remove unnecessary third-party scripts.

Largest Contentful Paint

Result: 4.0 s

Why: The main visible content is taking 4.0 s to appear. A slow Largest Contentful Paint usually means the main image, heading, or other large element is loading too late.

How to fix: Optimize the main hero image, use WebP or AVIF, preload the LCP resource when appropriate, reduce render-blocking resources, and defer non-critical JavaScript.

Cumulative Layout Shift

Result: 0.411

Why: Page elements are moving unexpectedly during loading (0.411). This can make the page difficult to use and can cause accidental clicks.

How to fix: Reserve space for images and advertisements, define width and height for media, avoid inserting content above existing content, and stabilize fonts before rendering.

Speed Index

Result: 5.2 s

Why: Visible page content is appearing slowly (5.2 s), so users may see an incomplete page for longer.

How to fix: Optimize above-the-fold images, reduce render-blocking resources, inline critical CSS, and defer non-critical assets.

UI / UX Analysis

https://goc.searchonic.online/

UI-001

Severity: Medium

Issue: Low contrast on supporting body copy

Why: The gray body paragraph text beneath the main headline provides low visual contrast against the pale cyan background.

Recommendation: Darken the body text color to a deeper slate/charcoal (#1e293b or darker) to ensure compliance with WCAG AA contrast standards.

UI-002

Severity: Low

Issue: Unanchored floating partner logo

Why: The Salesforce cloud logo floats above the imagery without a partnership label, badge container, or context.

Recommendation: Place the Salesforce logo inside a dedicated partner badge or accreditation lockup (e.g., 'Certified Salesforce Partner') to establish clear trust credentials.

UI-003

Severity: Low

Issue: Competing CTA button styles

Why: The header CTA ('Let's Talk') and the hero primary CTA ('Book a Free Consultation') share the exact same blue background styling, diluting the hierarchy of the main conversion path.

Recommendation: Style the navigation CTA as an outline/secondary button to ensure the hero CTA remains the distinct primary focal point.

https://goc.searchonic.online/about

UI-101

Severity: High

Issue: Poor button contrast on dark blue hero

Why: The purple-blue 'Connect Now' CTA button blends directly into the saturated blue background banner, failing visual pop and contrast requirements.

Recommendation: Use a high-contrast accent color (such as white with dark text or a bright accent color) to make the primary button easily distinguishable on dark backgrounds.

UI-102

Severity: Medium

Issue: Misaligned page intent and CTA messaging

Why: The 'About Us' hero banner leads directly with a sales pitch ('Connect with us to implement Salesforce solutions') and a conversion button ('Connect Now') rather than establishing company background, mission, or identity.

Recommendation: Structure the About page hero to highlight company vision, team expertise, and credibility before introducing direct contact actions.

UI-103

Severity: Low

Issue: Hard visual background separation

Why: The dark solid blue hero section abruptly transitions into a pale lilac/white section without visual transition or cohesive section framing.

Recommendation: Introduce softer section dividers, subtle gradients, or consistent background tonality aligned with the rest of the site.

https://goc.searchonic.online/revenue-cloud-implementation

Severity: High

Issue: Gibberish and placeholder UI text in visual graphics

Why: The floating analytics cards contain nonsensical dummy labels ('Tolis Revenue', 'This Guster', 'Bodee', 'Aotieilies by Status'), degrading professional trust and authenticity.

Recommendation: Replace dummy graphic text with realistic B2B revenue metrics (e.g., 'Total Revenue', 'This Quarter', 'Activities by Status', 'Pipeline Stage').

UI-202

Severity: Medium

Issue: Excessively long CTA label text

Why: The button label 'Start Your Revenue Cloud Implementation' is overly wordy, creating a wide button that hurts readability and scan speed.

Recommendation: Shorten the CTA copy to concise, action-oriented text such as 'Get Started' or 'Implement Revenue Cloud'.

UI-203

Severity: Low

Issue: Low contrast breadcrumb link text

Why: The teal/blue link text in the breadcrumb path provides weak contrast against the light cyan hero background.

Recommendation: Darken breadcrumb links to a higher-contrast blue or charcoal tone to meet WCAG AA legibility standards.
